# ERYD RHIVAMAR - ADLANTRIM ORC DYNASTIC HISTORY
# Generated against tfw_landed_titles(2).txt and tfw_forodwaith_titles(3).txt
#
# Design:
# - culture = adlantrim
# - religion = faith_unbowed
# - 12 original Orcs share one founding dynasty.
# - tfw_lineofadlantrim_mornaer_001 is the Orc Father and remains alive through 7000.
# - all later ruling/spousal branches descend from the original twelve.
# - lifespan targets use exponential decay from ~3000 years (generation 1)
#   to ~215 years (generation 12).
# - generation 12 is the current ruling generation at 6787.
# - generation 13 is born after 6787; generations 14-15 extend the lines toward 7100.
# - names are drawn from the existing elven-character name tokens where possible.
############################################################
